1: # OTA-WIDE-SKY130
2: 
3: Design a continuous-time analog OTA meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
4: Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS` and `.ends DUT`.
5: Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
6: Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
7: You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
8: No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
9: Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.
10: 
11: ## Time accounting
12: 
13: The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

1: # Public measurement contract: OTA-WIDE-SKY130
2: 
3: These methods and selected spec.json limits are implementation requirements.
4: Native validation is not implied by this document or an offline test result.
5: No method refers to a DUT-internal node or device role. A follower window is not
6: independent ICMR or distortion-qualified output swing.
7: 
8: ## DC, ports and operating point
9: 
10: Connect output to VINN externally, VINP to the input source, CL to external ground.
11: Meter all six DUT ports with zero-volt sources pointing INTO DUT. Save voltages,
12: signed currents, KCL residual and the sum of VSS-relative terminal powers.
13: An ideal output clamp must not create a false operating point. IREF is noiseless,
14: AC zero, constant in supply-ripple tests and enters IBIAS from VSS.
15: 
16: Sweep the card's input interval at 5 mV spacing, including both endpoints. Retain
17: all samples; take maximum absolute follower error and maximum signed-port power
18: only when every required sample is valid. Input loading and IBIAS compliance also
19: apply at every DC point. Save the independently solved output at all step endpoints.
20: Device diagnostics are optional; their absence is not a functional failure.
21: A native DC solution alone is not proof of temporal stability.
22: 
23: ## External-loop return ratio
24: 
25: Keep DC feedback and the real CL. e is the inverting-input side and f the output
26: side. Probe V(e)-V(f) with a DC-zero voltage source; inject DC-zero current from
27: VSS to e. Run voltage injection (V=1, I=0) and current injection (V=0, I=1) in one
28: ngspice process. Normalize actual source phasors and use i_f=-I(Vprobe):
29: 
30: A=i_f/Iinj and C=V(e,VSS)/Iinj in the current run;
31: B=i_f/Vinj and D=V(e,VSS)/Vinj in the voltage run.
32: 
33: L = [2(AD-BC)-A+D] / [2(BC-AD)+A-D+1].
34: 
35: Use the 1+L sign convention, not an automatically sign-flipped curve. Preserve
36: both raw paths and identical frequency grids/DC/model realization. Numerical
37: denominator cancellation uses the prescribed relative guard 1e-10; unresolved
38: values cannot become infinite gain or a passing margin. Record the ratio of
39: absolute denominator to the sum of its term magnitudes.
40: 
41: Sweep 10 Hz-1 GHz at 50 points/decade for SKY130. All samples finite,
42: frequencies increasing, endpoint relative error <=1e-7 and log10 spacing
43: <=0.020001. Low-frequency gain is 20 log10(abs(L)) at 10 Hz, not an invented DC
44: limit. Reuse all-crossing extraction: interpolate linearly in log frequency,
45: select the first positive-to-negative 0 dB crossing, unwrap phase from the first
46: principal phase with adjacent changes within +/-180 degrees. Report each unity
47: crossing's frequency, unwrapped phase and gain direction, plus touches/boundaries.
48: Record whether any later sample exceeds 0 dB + 1e-6 dB after the selected crossing.
49: These diagnostics have no PASS/MISS verdict. Do not
50: convert the crossing phase into a phase margin or report a gain-margin scalar.
51: Only the 10 Hz gain and first descending crossing frequency are scored here.
52: A missing selected crossing cannot pass the bandwidth requirement. Invalid or incomplete
53: AC data remains a measurement failure.
54: 
55: This two-port measurement is an external-loop metric. It does not certify every
56: internal feedback mode of an arbitrary circuit. Time-domain requirements remain
57: separate; global internal-mode certification is not a new acceptance gate.
58: Method basis: Tian et al., Fig. 7 and eqs. 21-23/30,
59: https://kenkundert.com/docs/cd2001-01.pdf .
60: 
61: ## CMRR: bounded DC balance, then two AC paths
62: 
63: Fix average input to the card's VCM and target VOUT=VCM. Set
64: VINP=VCM+VD0/2 and VINN=VCM-VD0/2 without output clamp or AC servo.
65: Try VD0=0 first. Success means abs(VOUT-VCM)<=10 uV at a valid OP. Otherwise
66: try endpoints -10 mV and +10 mV. Accept an endpoint meeting tolerance or require
67: a negative/positive residual bracket in that order. Bisect at most 32 times,
68: retaining each VD0, VOUT and status. There are at most 35 explicit OP commands.
69: Failure to bracket/converge is NO_VALID_BALANCED_OP / BALANCE_UNRESOLVED, not
70: proof of topology infeasibility. No adaptive range expansion or silent bias fix.
71: 
72: At the successful unclamped OP keep VD0 fixed. Differential AC is +0.5/-0.5 V
73: (unit difference); common AC is +1/+1 V (unit common mode). Other sources are AC
74: zero. At exactly 1 kHz, report 20 log10(abs(Ad/Acm)), the input phasors and both
75: paths. Zero/underflow/nonfinite ratios or a missing valid OP are invalid evidence,
76: not automatic infinite rejection.
77: 
78: Perform the bounded control loop and both AC paths in ONE native process and log
79: explicit OP count and implicit AC operating-point work. Timeout stops that case;
80: no unlimited loop or free process retries. The offline callback is an algorithm
81: oracle, not permission to count 35 separate processes as one. The official ngspice
82: control tutorial documents alter/op/loop flows and multiple analyses in one run:
83: https://ngspice.sourceforge.io/ngspice-control-language-tutorial.html .
84: 
85: ## Transients
86: 
87: Use card-specific levels, edge start/duration, stop time and maximum sample gap.
88: Each transient uses its own independently solved DC endpoints. Measure slew from
89: edge start: an output which crosses 20/80% during the finite input edge remains
90: valid. Require complete finite increasing samples throughout the observation.
91: Small up/down tests cover the center (0.70/0.75 V), low (0.25/0.30 V), and
92: high (1.05/1.10 V) regions in separate native processes. A nominal group costs 11
93: cases; step costs nine including DC. Full verification is 210 public plus 108
94: independent cases. All six small steps
95: are scored. This joint input/output follower test is not independent ICMR.
96: 
97: Settling is final interpolated entry that remains within 1% of INPUT step amplitude
98: about the independently solved destination DC OUTPUT, measured from edge start.
99: DC tracking is a separate required check. Wrong static levels therefore cannot
100: pass by tail normalization. Complete unsettled data yields null settling and an
101: observation-time lower bound; a bound beyond the limit is MISS. Short or missing
102: waveforms are invalid, not fast settling. Small-step overshoot is excursion beyond
103: target divided by absolute independent DC output transition. Large-step slew uses
104: the first ordered direction-correct 20/80% crossings after the edge starts. Retain
105: recrossings and characterize large-step overshoot; don't add a new waveform score.
106: 
107: ## Noise, PSRR and verdicts
108: 
109: Noise uses the real follower at VCM and the same-circuit signal transfer. Save
110: input-referred ASD, integrate its squared PSD in linear frequency with trapezoids
111: and boundary interpolation, then square root: 10 Hz-10 MHz at 50 points/decade.
112: Keep 10/100/1000 Hz lower-cutoff integrals as diagnostics.
113: 
114: PSRR uses two AC paths at the same follower OP: VDD=1/input=0 then VDD=0/input=1.
115: IREF and VCM do not follow supply ripple. Save phasors and calculate
116: 20 log10(abs(Hsignal/Hsupply)) at 1 kHz and 1 MHz, not an LDO supply/output-only
117: formula. Invalid/zero transfers do not become favorable zero noise/infinite PSRR.
118: 
119: All required standard cases must produce valid passing metrics on one DUT.
120: Inclusive limits are compared without rounding. Keep failed metrics and unknowns;
121: partial execution is not a completed PASS or a substituted last-good design.
122: Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
123: Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
124: A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

Read file /workspace/CLIENT.md, lines 1-44
1: # Shared client
2: 
3: Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
4: Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
5: `atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
6: `atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
7: `atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
8: Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
9: The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
10: Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
11: Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
12: Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.
13: 
14: ## Public measurement options
15: 
16: | Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
17: |---|---|---:|---:|---|
18: | nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 11 | dc, loop, small_up, small_down, large_up, large_down, noise, small_low_up, small_low_down, small_high_up, small_high_down |
19: | nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 11 | dc, loop, small_up, small_down, large_up, large_down, noise, small_low_up, small_low_down, small_high_up, small_high_down |
20: | dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | dc |
21: | dc | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | dc |
22: | loop | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | loop |
23: | loop | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | loop |
24: | step | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 9 | dc, small_up, small_down, large_up, large_down, small_low_up, small_low_down, small_high_up, small_high_down |
25: | step | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 9 | dc, small_up, small_down, large_up, large_down, small_low_up, small_low_down, small_high_up, small_high_down |
26: | noise | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-12 | 1 | noise |
27: | noise | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 5e-12 | 1 | noise |
28: | psrr | N, SLH, FHC | 2e-12 | 1 | psrr |
29: | psrr | N, SLH, FHC | 5e-12 | 1 | psrr |
30: | cmrr | N, SLH, FHC | 2e-12 | 1 | cmrr |
31: | cmrr | N, SLH, FHC | 5e-12 | 1 | cmrr |
32: 
33: Defaults and seed requirements:
34: 
35: - nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
36: - dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
37: - loop: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
38: - step: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
39: - noise: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
40: - psrr: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
41: - cmrr: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-12 F. No seed accepted.
42: 
43: One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
44: `estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

/usr/local/bin/atb-client: Python script, ASCII text executable
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,a

I've read the task, spec, measurement contract, and devices grammar. Key facts:

  • sky130 only, W must lie in specific bands (NFET W≥0.36, PFET W≥0.42), L≥0.15.
  • Wide supply (1.35–1.65 V), input range 0.25–1.1 V with VCM=0.75 ⇒ rail-to-rail input is mandatory (neither a single N-pair nor P-pair covers that range).
  • Follower-based DC/loop/step/noise, CMRR/PSRR at N/SLH/FHC, CL = 2–5 pF.
  • No local ngspice, so the client is my only simulator.

Let me write a first 2-stage Miller OTA with complementary (N+P) input pairs summing into one high-impedance node, and validate the netlist grammar.

def g(x,*ks):
    for k in ks:
        if isinstance(x,dict) and k in x: x=x[k]
        else: return None
    return x

        if isinstance(v,(int,float,str,bool)) or v is None:
            print('  ',k,'=',v)

            print('  ',k,':',json.dumps(v)[:600])
        else:
            print('  ',k,':',str(v)[:300])

        if pts and 'input_v' in pts[0]:

MODEL
0:10:45

The DC loss at the high end is a headroom mechanism: the second-stage gate level (na2 = VDD−Vsg11) must not be required below the N-pair's saturation limit (ntail+Vdsat at Vin=1.1). With M11 at W/L=100 its Vov is too large, pushing na2 too low. Fix: make M11's Vov match the first-stage mirror (W/L = 10×W/L(M3) = 400). I'll apply that and run a batch of measurements in one turn:

/workspace/tools/summ2.py
content
import json,sys,glob,os
FILES=sys.argv[1:]
KEYS=['tracking_max_error_v','power_w','input_current_abs_a','ibias_compliant','loop_gain_db_at_ref','selected_unity_gain_hz','input_noise_rms_v','settling_time_s','overshoot_ratio','slope_v_per_s','status','valid']
for f in FILES:
    try: d=json.load(open(f))
    except Exception as e: print(f,'LOADERR',e); continue
    print('#'*80)
    print(os.path.basename(f), d.get('analysis'), d.get('pvt'), 'cl=',d.get('cl'), 'rc=',d.get('returncode'), 'elapsed',round(d.get('elapsed_s',0),1))
    for c in d.get('cases',[]):
        cid=c.get('case_id'); m=c.get('measurements',{})
        row=[]
        if 'tracking_max_error_v' in m:
            row.append('trkerr=%.3g'%m['tracking_max_error_v'])
            row.append('P=%.0fuW'%(m['power_w']*1e6))
        if 'loop_gain_db_at_ref' in m:
            row.append('L0=%.1fdB'%m['loop_gain_db_at_ref'])
            row.append('UGF=%.3gMHz'%(m['selected_unity_gain_hz']/1e6))
            row.append('recross=%s'%m.get('no_0db_recross'))
            uc=m.get('unity_gain_crossings') or []
            if uc: row.append('ph=%.1f'%uc[0].get('phase_deg',0))
        if 'input_noise_rms_v' in m: row.append('noise=%.1fuV'%(m['input_noise_rms_v']*1e6))
        if 'settling_time_s' in m:
            st=m['settling_time_s']; row.append('ts=%s'%('%.3gns'%(st*1e9) if st is not None else 'None'))
            row.append('OS=%.2f%%'%(100*(m.get('overshoot_ratio') or 0)))
            tr=m.get('transit_20_80') or {}
            if tr.get('slope_v_per_s'): row.append('SR=%.2fV/us'%(tr['slope_v_per_s']/1e6))
        print(f"  {cid:16s} {c.get('status'):4s} "+' '.join(row))
    # worst dc error point for dc cases
    for c in d.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        if c.get('case_id')=='dc' and 'points' in m and isinstance(m['points'][0],dict):
            pts=m['points']
            errs=sorted((abs(p['output_v']-p['input_v']),p['input_v'],p['output_v']) for p in pts)
            print('   dc worst:',[ (round(e,5),iv,round(ov,5)) for e,iv,ov in errs[:3]])

The DC failure at SLC is a systematic offset mechanism (Vout pins at VDD−0.46 V). Let me run targeted variants at SLC in a single batch to identify the limiter:

ACTION
0:14:13

content
import subprocess, json, sys, os, re

BASE = open('/workspace/dut_base.txt').read()

def variant(name, subs):
    txt = BASE
    nm = 0
    for old,new in subs:
        assert old in txt, (name, old)
        txt = txt.replace(old,new)
        nm += 1
    return txt

def run(name, txt, pvt, cl, analysis='dc'):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    eid = 't-%s-%s' % (name,pvt)
    p = subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id',eid,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt,'--cl',cl],
                       capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    try:
        d = json.loads(p.stdout)
    except Exception as e:
        print(name,pvt,'BADJSON',p.stdout[:300]); return
    if not d.get('ok'):
        print(name,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:300]); return
    for c in d['cases']:
        m = c.get('measurements',{})
        e = m.get('tracking_max_error_v')
        pts = m.get('points') or []
        worst = max(pts, key=lambda p: abs(p['output_v']-p['input_v'])) if pts else {}
        print('%-10s %-4s cases=%d trkerr=%.4f  worst@vin=%.2f vout=%.4f P=%.1fuW' % (
            name, pvt, len(d['cases']), (e if e is not None else -1),
            worst.get('input_v',0), worst.get('output_v',0), 1e6*(m.get('power_w',0))))
    return d

if __name__=='__main__':
    base = BASE
    run('base','SLC','2e-12',base)
    # T1: output current halved
    run('m12x5', variant('m12x5',[('Xm12 VOUT IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=10',
                                   'Xm12 VOUT IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=5')]),'SLC','2e-12')
    # T2: M11 doubled
    run('m11x8', variant('m11x8',[('Xm11 VOUT na2 VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=1 nf=1 m=4',
                                   'Xm11 VOUT na2 VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=100 L=1 nf=1 m=8')]),'SLC','2e-12')
    # T3: input pair narrower (higher Vgs1)
    run('pairx2', variant('pairx2',[('Xm1 na1 VINN ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4',
                                     'Xm1 na1 VINN ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2'),
                                   ('Xm2 na2 VINP ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4',
                                     'Xm2 na2 VINP ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2')]),'SLC','2e-12')
    # T4: N tail doubled
    run('tailx4', variant('tailx4',[('Xm14 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2',
                                     'Xm14 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4')]),'SLC','2e-12')

content
import subprocess, json, sys, os

BASE = open('/workspace/dut_base.txt').read()

def run(name, txt, pvt='SLC', cl='2e-12'):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    eid = 'b2-%s-%s' % (name,pvt)
    p = subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id',eid,'--analysis','dc','--pvt',pvt,'--cl',cl],
                       capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d = json.loads(p.stdout) if p.stdout.strip().startswith('{') else {'ok':False,'raw':p.stdout[:200]}
    if not d.get('ok'):
        print(name,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:200]); return None
    c = d['cases'][0]; m = c['measurements']
    pts = m.get('points') or []
    print('%-9s %-4s rc=%s trkerr=%.4f' % (name,pvt,c.get('solver_status'),m.get('tracking_max_error_v',-1)), end='  ')
    row=[]
    for p_ in pts:
        iv = p_['input_v']
        if iv in (0.25,) or abs(iv-0.8)<1e-9 or abs(iv-0.9)<1e-9 or abs(iv-1.0)<1e-9 or abs(iv-1.1)<1e-9:
            row.append('%.2f->%.3f'%(iv,p_['output_v']))
    print(' | '.join(row))
    return d

if __name__=='__main__':
    # control base
    run('base', BASE)
    # A: N-pair only (remove P-pair M6,M7 and its mirror M8,M9 and P-tail M10)
    import re
    A = BASE
    for pat in [r'Xm6 [^\n]*\n', r'Xm7 [^\n]*\n', r'Xm8 [^\n]*\n', r'Xm9 [^\n]*\n', r'Xm10 [^\n]*\n']:
        A = re.sub(pat,'',A)
    run('nonly', A)
    # B: first stage only -> VOUT = na2, remove M11,M12,Rz,Cc
    B = BASE
    for pat in [r'Xm11 [^\n]*\n', r'Xm12 [^\n]*\n', r'Rz [^\n]*\n', r'Cc [^\n]*\n']:
        B = re.sub(pat,'',B)
    run('fsonly', B)
    # D: weaker P-mirror M9 (m=4 -> m=1)
    D = BASE.replace('Xm9 na2 nb1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4',
                     'Xm9 na2 nb1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=1')
    run('m9weak', D)
    # E: P-tail halved (M10 m=2 -> m=1)
    E = BASE.replace('Xm10 ptail pbias VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2',
                     'Xm10 ptail pbias VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=1')
    run('ptailhalf', E)

---

def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
---

import subprocess, json, os, re

BASE = open('/workspace/dut_base.txt').read()

def run(name, txt, pvt='SLC', cl='2e-12', show=True):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    eid = 'b3-%s-%s' % (name,pvt)
    p = subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id',eid,'--analysis','dc','--pvt',pvt,'--cl',cl],
                       capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d = json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'): print(name,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:200]); return
    c=d['cases'][0]; m=c['measurements']; pts=m['points']
    err=m['tracking_max_error_v']
    print('== %-10s %s trkerr=%.4f  P=%.1fuW'%(name,pvt,err,m['power_w']*1e6))
    if show:
        row=[]
        for p_ in pts:
            iv=p_['input_v']
            k=round(iv*1000)
            if k%25==0 and iv>=0.5:
                row.append('%.3f:%.3f'%(iv,p_['output_v']))
        print('   '+' '.join(row))

run('base', BASE)

Key insight needed: I can infer device operating points from the reported port power. Let me characterize the actual SKY130 Vth/µCox at the corners by making diode-connected test DUTs (power = I×V):

ACTION
0:19:32
write · devchar.py
/workspace/tools/devchar.py
content
import subprocess, json

HDR='.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS\n'
FTR='.ends DUT\n'
LEAK='Rv VOUT VSS 10meg\nRw VINN VSS 10meg\n'

def dev(pol,w,m):
    if pol=='n':
        return 'Xm IBIAS IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=%d L=1 nf=1 m=%d\n'%(w,m)
    return 'Xm IBIAS IBIAS VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=%d L=1 nf=1 m=%d\n'%(w,m)

def measure(net,tag,pvt):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(net)
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id','dev-%s-%s'%(tag,pvt),'--analysis','dc','--pvt',pvt,'--cl','2e-12'],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d=json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'):
        print(tag,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:200]); return None
    m=d['cases'][0]['measurements']
    P=m['power_w']
    I=10e-6
    if tag[0]=='n':  V= P/I            # NMOS diode: P = V(IBIAS)*10uA
    else:            V= 1.5 - P/I      # PMOS diode: P = (VDD-V(IBIAS))*10uA  (VDD=1.5/1.35/1.65!)
    return P,V

def vdd_of(pvt):
    return {'N':1.5,'SLC':1.35,'SLH':1.35,'SHC':1.65,'SHH':1.65,'FLC':1.35,'FLH':1.35,'FHC':1.65,'FHH':1.65}[pvt]

if __name__=='__main__':
    pvts=['N','SLC','FLH','FHC']
    for pvt in pvts:
        for pol,w,m,I in [('n',10,1,10e-6),('n',10,4,40e-6),('p',20,1,10e-6),('p',20,4,40e-6)]:
            net=HDR+dev(pol,w,m)+LEAK+FTR
            r=measure(net,'%s%d_%d'%(pol,w,m),pvt)
            if r is None: continue
            P,V=r
            # for PMOS correct with actual vdd
            if pol=='p': V=vdd_of(pvt)-P/10e-6
            vth_est=''
            print('%-4s %s W=%d m=%d  P=%.2fuW  Vport=%.4f'%(pvt,pol,w,m,P*1e6,V))

The IBIAS direction is confirmed (current into the pin), so only NMOS diode measurements via IBIAS are valid. I'll use a resistor-loaded diode method (VDD-port current readable from power) to characterize both device types at the SLC corner:

content
import subprocess, json

HDR='.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS\n'
FTR='.ends DUT\n'
LEAK='Rv VOUT VSS 10meg\nRw VINN VSS 10meg\nRi IBIAS VSS 10meg\n'

def nfet(w,l,m): return 'sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=%s L=%s nf=1 m=%s'%(w,l,m)
def pfet(w,l,m): return 'sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=%s L=%s nf=1 m=%s'%(w,l,m)

def run(net,tag,pvt):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(net)
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id','dv-%s-%s'%(tag,pvt),'--analysis','dc','--pvt',pvt,'--cl','2e-12'],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d=json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'): print(tag,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:200]); return
    m=d['cases'][0]['measurements']
    P=m['power_w']
    VDD={'N':1.5,'SLC':1.35,'SLH':1.35,'SHC':1.65,'SHH':1.65,'FLC':1.35,'FLH':1.35,'FHC':1.65,'FHH':1.65}[pvt]
    I=P/VDD
    vp=m['points'][0].get('output_v')  # not useful
    return I,P,VDD

def test(pol,w,l,m,R,pvt):
    if pol=='n':
        body='Rd VDD P %s\nXd P P VSS VSS %s\n'%(R,nfet(w,l,m))
    else:
        body='Rd P VSS %s\nXd P P VDD VDD %s\n'%(R,pfet(w,l,m))
    r=run(HDR+body+LEAK+FTR,'%s%sx%s_%s_%s'%(pol,w,l,m,R),pvt)
    if r is None: return
    I,P,VDD=r
    vterm=D['pts'][-1] if False else None
    # component voltage at node P: for nmos Vgs = VP (0..VDD), pmos Vsg = VDD-VP
    VP = VDD - I*float(R) if pol=='n' else I*float(R)
    Vg = VP if pol=='n' else VDD-VP
    print('%-4s %s W/L=%s/%s m=%s R=%-6s I=%8.3fuA  Vgs_or_Vsg=%.4f  (VP=%.4f)'%(pvt,pol,w,l,m,R,I*1e6,Vg,VP))

if __name__=='__main__':
    import sys
    for pvt in ['N','SLC']:
        test('n','10','1','4','100k',pvt)
        test('n','10','1','4','22k',pvt)
        test('n','10','1','2','47k',pvt)
        test('n','10','1','10','8.2k',pvt)
        test('p','100','1','4','10k',pvt)
        test('p','100','1','4','3.3k',pvt)
        test('p','10','1','4','100k',pvt)
        test('p','10','1','4','33k',pvt)

import subprocess, json

HDR='.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS\n'
FTR='.ends DUT\n'
LEAK='Rv VOUT VSS 10meg\nRw VINN VSS 10meg\nRi IBIAS VSS 10meg\n'
VDDMAP={'N':1.5,'SLC':1.35,'SLH':1.35,'SHC':1.65,'SHH':1.65,'FLC':1.35,'FLH':1.35,'FHC':1.65,'FHH':1.65}

def nfet(w,l,m): return 'sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=%s L=%s nf=1 m=%s'%(w,l,m)
def pfet(w,l,m): return 'sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=%s L=%s nf=1 m=%s'%(w,l,m)

def run(net,tag,pvt):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(net)
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id','dv-%s-%s'%(tag,pvt),'--analysis','dc','--pvt',pvt,'--cl','2e-12'],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d=json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'): print(tag,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:200]); return None
    return d['cases'][0]['measurements']['power_w']

def test(pol,w,l,m,Rstr,rohm,pvt):
    if pol=='n':
        body='Rd VDD P %s\nXd P P VSS VSS %s\n'%(Rstr,nfet(w,l,m))
    else:
        body='Rd P VSS %s\nXd P P VDD VDD %s\n'%(Rstr,pfet(w,l,m))
    P=run(HDR+body+LEAK+FTR,'%s%sx%s_%s_%s'%(pol,w,l,m,Rstr),pvt)
    if P is None: return
    VDD=VDDMAP[pvt]; I=P/VDD
    if pol=='n': VP = VDD - I*rohm
    else:        VP = I*rohm
    Vg = VP if pol=='n' else VDD-VP
    print('%-4s %s W=%s L=%s m=%s R=%-6s I=%8.3fuA  Vgs/Vsg=%.4f (VP=%.4f)'%(pvt,pol,w,l,m,Rstr,I*1e6,Vg,VP))

if __name__=='__main__':
    for pvt in ['N','SLC']:
        test('n','10','1','4','100k',100e3,pvt)
        test('n','10','1','4','22k',22e3,pvt)
        test('n','10','1','2','47k',47e3,pvt)
        test('n','10','1','10','8.2k',8.2e3,pvt)
        test('p','100','1','4','10k',10e3,pvt)
        test('p','100','1','4','3.3k',3.3e3,pvt)
        test('p','10','1','4','100k',100e3,pvt)
        test('p','10','1','4','33k',33e3,pvt)

0:27:01

I now have the crucial device data for this process: |Vth_p| ≈ 0.9–1.05 V, |Vth_n| ≈ 0.5–0.6 V, µCox_p ≈ 23 µA/V², µCox_n ≈ 100 µA/V². That explains everything: the v2 PMOS second stage needs its gate at ≤ VDD−1.0 V, which collides with the NMOS input pair's tail node at Vin=1.1. The fix is architectural: put the diff-to-single conversion mirror at the bottom (NMOS) and use PMOS current-source loads on top (their sources sit at VDD), plus an NMOS common-source output stage. Writing v3:

ACTION
0:26:59

/workspace/dut.spice
content
* OTA-WIDE-SKY130 v3 : 2-stage, complementary input, NMOS bottom mirror,
* PMOS current-source loads (sources at VDD), NMOS common-source 2nd stage
.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS

* ---- bias from IBIAS (10uA into diode) ----
Xm20 IBIAS IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=1
Xm21 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2
Xm22 pbiasn IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=1
Xm23 pbiasn pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=1
Xm24 ptail pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2
Xm25 VOUT pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=10

* ---- N input pair: PMOS current-source loads, NMOS mirror to node B ----
Xm3 A pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2
Xm4 B pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2
Xm1 A VINN ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4
Xm2 B VINP ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4
Xm8 A A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=5 nf=1 m=1
Xm9 B A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=5 nf=1 m=1

* ---- P input pair with NMOS mirror to node B ----
Xm6 E VINN ptail VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4
Xm7 B VINP ptail VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=4
Xm13 E E VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=5 nf=1 m=1
Xm14 B E VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=5 nf=1 m=1

* ---- second stage: NMOS common source ----
Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2

* ---- Miller compensation ----
Rz B nz 1.5k
Cc nz VOUT 5p

.ends DUT

import json,sys
for f in sys.argv[1:]:
    d=json.load(open(f))
    for c in d['cases']:
        if c['case_id']!='dc': continue
        m=c['measurements']; pts=m['points']
        errs=sorted(((abs(p['output_v']-p['input_v']),p['input_v'],p['output_v']) for p in pts),reverse=True)
        print('== %s %s trkerr=%.4f top:'%(f,d.get('pvt'),m['tracking_max_error_v']),
              [(round(e,4),round(iv,3),round(ov,4)) for e,iv,ov in errs[:3]])
        row=[]
        for p in pts:
            iv=p['input_v']
            if iv>=0.9 and abs(round(iv*20)-iv*20)<1e-9 and round(iv*20)%2==0:
                row.append('%.3f:%.4f(%+.4f)'%(iv,p['output_v'],p['output_v']-iv))
        if row: print('   hi:',' '.join(row))
        row=[]
        for p in pts:
            iv=p['input_v']
            if 0.28<=iv<=0.8 and abs(round(iv*20)-iv*20)<1e-9:
                row.append('%.2f:%.4f(%+.4f)'%(iv,p['output_v'],p['output_v']-iv))
        if row: print('   mid:',' '.join(row))

0:34:45

The v4 regression came from a bigger A↔B level mismatch driving current-mismatch into the high-impedance node. Key design principle discovered: match current densities so the mirror-diode node (A), the first-stage output (B) and the 2nd-stage gate all sit at the same voltage at every corner (kills the systematic offset). Applying v5:

ACTION
0:34:43
write · dut.spice
/workspace/dut.spice
content
* OTA-WIDE-SKY130 v5 : complementary input, NMOS bottom mirror, PMOS current
* source loads, NMOS CS 2nd stage. Density-matched mirror nodes.
.subckt DUT VINP VINN VOUT VDD VSS IBIAS

* ---- bias from IBIAS (10uA into diode) ----
Xm20 IBIAS IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=1
Xm21 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2
Xm22 pbiasn IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=1
Xm23 pbiasn pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=1
Xm24 ptail pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=2
Xm25 VOUT pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=0.5 nf=1 m=4

* ---- N input pair: PMOS current-source loads, NMOS mirror to node B ----
Xm3 A pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=2
Xm4 B pbiasn VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=2
Xm1 A VINN ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=32
Xm2 B VINP ntail VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=32
Xm8 A A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=7.5 nf=1 m=1
Xm9 B A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=7.5 nf=1 m=1

* ---- P input pair with NMOS mirror to node B ----
Xm6 E VINN ptail VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=32
Xm7 B VINP ptail VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=32
Xm13 E E VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=7.5 nf=1 m=1
Xm14 B E VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=7.5 nf=1 m=1

* ---- second stage: NMOS common source ----
Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 nf=1 m=2

* ---- Miller compensation ----
Rz B nz 1.2k
Cc nz VOUT 6p

.ends DUT

ACTION
0:57:58
write · noisevar.py
/workspace/tools/noisevar.py
content
import subprocess, json, re

BASE = open('/workspace/dut.spice').read()

def run(name, txt, pvt='SLH', analysis='noise', cl='2e-12'):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id','nz-%s-%s'%(name,pvt),'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt,'--cl',cl],
                     capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d=json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'): print(name,pvt,'FAIL',json.dumps(d)[:150]); return
    m=d['cases'][0]['measurements']
    v=m.get('input_noise_rms_v')
    extra=''
    if 'loop_gain_db_at_ref' in m: extra='L0=%.1f UGF=%.2fMHz'%(m['loop_gain_db_at_ref'],m['selected_unity_gain_hz']/1e6)
    if 'tracking_max_error_v' in m: extra='trkerr=%.4f P=%.0fuW'%(m['tracking_max_error_v'],m['power_w']*1e6)
    print('%-8s %s %s %.1fuV %s'%(name,pvt,analysis,(v or 0)*1e6,extra))

def var(**kw):
    s=BASE
    if kw.get('iout'):  # M25 ratio and M11 size
        if kw['iout']==30:
            s=s.replace('Xm25 VOUT pbiaso VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=6',
                        'Xm25 VOUT pbiaso VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=3')
            s=s.replace('Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=5 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1',
                        'Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=2.5 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1')
            s=s.replace('Xm8 A A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=6 nf=1 m=1',
                        'Xm8 A A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=9 nf=1 m=1')
            s=s.replace('Xm9 B A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=6 nf=1 m=1',
                        'Xm9 B A VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=9 nf=1 m=1')
    if kw.get('tail40'):
        s=s.replace('Xm21 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=2',
                    'Xm21 ntail IBIAS VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=20 L=1 nf=1 m=4')
        s=s.replace('Xm3 A pbiasb VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=2',
                    'Xm3 A pbiasb VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=3')
        s=s.replace('Xm4 B pbiasb VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=2',
                    'Xm4 B pbiasb VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=40 L=2 nf=1 m=3')
    return s

if __name__=='__main__':
    run('base',BASE)
    run('iout30',var(iout=30))
    run('iout30t40',var(iout=30,tail40=True))
    run('tail40',var(tail40=True))

content
import json,sys,glob,os
LIM=dict(track=0.01,power=0.0006,noise=0.00015,L0=50,ugf=3e6,ts_small=5e-7,ts_large=1e-6,os=0.10,slew=2e6)
rows=[]
for f in sorted(glob.glob('res_v10-*.json'))+sorted(glob.glob('res_v9n-*.json')):
    d=json.load(open(f))
    pvt=d.get('pvt'); cl=d.get('cl')
    row={'pvt':pvt,'cl':cl,'file':os.path.basename(f),'fails':[]}
    for c in d.get('cases',[]):
        cid=c['case_id']; m=c.get('measurements',{})
        if cid=='dc':
            e=m.get('tracking_max_error_v'); row['trk']=e
            if e is None or e>LIM['track']: row['fails'].append('dc')
        if cid=='loop':
            row['L0']=m.get('loop_gain_db_at_ref'); row['ugf']=m.get('selected_unity_gain_hz')
            if row['L0'] is None or row['L0']<LIM['L0']: row['fails'].append('L0')
            if row['ugf'] is None or row['ugf']<LIM['ugf']: row['fails'].append('ugf')
            if m.get('no_0db_recross')!=1: row['fails'].append('recross')
        if cid=='noise':
            row['noise']=m.get('input_noise_rms_v')
            if row['noise'] is None or row['noise']>LIM['noise']: row['fails'].append('noise')
        if cid.startswith('small') or cid.startswith('large'):
            ts=m.get('settling_time_s'); osr=m.get('overshoot_ratio'); tr=m.get('transit_20_80') or {}
            lim=LIM['ts_small'] if cid.startswith('small') else LIM['ts_large']
            if ts is None or ts>lim: row['fails'].append('ts:'+cid)
            if cid.startswith('small') and osr is not None and osr>LIM['os']: row['fails'].append('os:'+cid)
            if cid.startswith('large') and tr.get('slope_v_per_s') is not None and tr['slope_v_per_s']<LIM['slew']: row['fails'].append('sr:'+cid)
        if 'power_w' in m and cid=='dc':
            row['P']=m['power_w']
            if m['power_w']>LIM['power']: row['fails'].append('power')
    rows.append(row)
print('%-6s %-6s %-22s %7s %7s %8s %8s %8s  %s'%('pvt','cl','file','trk_mV','P_uW','L0_dB','UGF_MHz','noise_uV','FAILS'))
for r in rows:
    print('%-6s %-6s %-22s %7s %7s %8s %8s %8s  %s'%(
        r['pvt'], ('%.0fp'%(float(r['cl'])*1e12)), r['file'],
        ('%.2f'%(r['trk']*1000)) if r.get('trk') is not None else '-',
        ('%.0f'%(r['P']*1e6)) if r.get('P') is not None else '-',
        ('%.1f'%r['L0']) if r.get('L0') is not None else '-',
        ('%.1f'%(r['ugf']/1e6)) if r.get('ugf') is not None else '-',
        ('%.0f'%(r['noise']*1e6)) if r.get('noise') is not None else '-',
        ','.join(r['fails']) if r['fails'] else 'PASS'))

import subprocess, json
BASE=open('dut_v10.txt').read()
def i120(s):
    s=s.replace('Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=5 L=0.5 nf=1 m=1',
                'Xm11 VOUT B VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=0.5 nf=1 m=2')
    s=s.replace('Xm25 VOUT pbiaso VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=6',
                'Xm25 VOUT pbiaso VDD VDD sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 nf=1 m=12')
    return s
def meas(txt,tag,analysis,pvt,cl='5e-12'):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(txt)
    p=subprocess.run(['atb-client','measure','--id',tag,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt,'--cl',cl],capture_output=True,text=True,cwd='/workspace')
    d=json.loads(p.stdout)
    if not d.get('ok'): print(tag,pvt,'FAIL',str(d)[:100]); return None
    return d['cases'][0]['measurements']
for name,txt in [('i120c5',i120(BASE)), ('i120c6',i120(BASE).replace('Cc nz VOUT 5p','Cc nz VOUT 6p'))]:

        m=meas(txt,'x-%s-%s'%(name,pvt),'loop',pvt)
        if m:
            uc=(m.get('unity_gain_crossings') or [{}])[0]

    m=meas(txt,'x-%s-noSLH'%name,'noise','SLH','2e-12')
    if m: print('%-8s SLH noise=%.1fuV'%(name,m['input_noise_rms_v']*1e6))

import json
for f in ['res_v11-FHC-2.json','res_v11-SHC-2.json','res_v11-FHC-5.json']:
    d=json.load(open(f)); print('==',f)
    for c in d['cases']:
        if not c['case_id'].startswith('small'): continue
        m=c['measurements']
        print('   %-15s OS=%5.2f%% ts=%.0fns'%(c['case_id'],100*(m.get('overshoot_ratio') or 0),(m['settling_time_s'] or 0)*1e9))
# SLC dc worst points
d=json.load(open('res_v11-SLC-2.json'))
for c in d['cases']:
    if c['case_id']=='dc':
        m=c['measurements']; pts=m['points']
        errs=sorted(((abs(p['output_v']-p['input_v']),p['input_v'],p['output_v']) for p in pts),reverse=True)

# nominal checks
for f in sorted(glob.glob('res_v12-*.json')):
    if 'psrr' in f or 'cmrr' in f: continue
    d=json.load(open(f)); fails=[]; kv={}
    for c in d['cases']:
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        if c['case_id']=='dc':
            kv['trk']=m['tracking_max_error_v']; kv['P']=m['power_w']
            if m['tracking_max_error_v']>0.01: fails.append('dc')
            if m['power_w']>0.0006: fails.append('power')
            if m.get('input_current_abs_a',0)>1e-6: fails.append('iin')
            if not m.get('ibias_compliant'): fails.append('ibias')
        if c['case_id']=='loop':
            kv['L0']=m['loop_gain_db_at_ref']; kv['ugf']=m['selected_unity_gain_hz']
            if m['loop_gain_db_at_ref']<50: fails.append('L0')
            if m['selected_unity_gain_hz']<3e6: fails.append('ugf_lo')
            if m.get('no_0db_recross')!=1: fails.append('recross')
        if c['case_id']=='noise':
            kv['noise']=m['input_noise_rms_v']
            if m['input_noise_rms_v']>1.5e-4: fails.append('noise')
        if c['case_id'].startswith('small'):
            if (m.get('settling_time_s') or 1)>5e-7: fails.append('ts:'+c['case_id'])
            if (m.get('overshoot_ratio') or 0)>0.10: fails.append('OS:%s %.1f%%'%(c['case_id'],100*m['overshoot_ratio']))
        if c['case_id'].startswith('large'):
            if (m.get('settling_time_s') or 1)>1e-6: fails.append('ts:'+c['case_id'])
            if ((m.get('transit_20_80') or {}).get('slope_v_per_s') or 9)<2e6: fails.append('SR')
    print('%-14s trk=%5.2fmV P=%3.0fuW L0=%4.1f UGF=%5.1f noise=%3.0fuV %s'%(
        f,kv.get('trk',0)*1000,kv.get('P',0)*1e6,kv.get('L0',0),kv.get('ugf',0)/1e6,kv.get('noise',0)*1e6,
        ('FAIL:'+','.join(fails)) if fails else 'PASS'))

ACTION
2:43:36
shell
cd /workspace && atb-client submit --rationale "Two-stage class-A OTA for SKY130 in 0.25-1.1V CM range, VDD 1.35-1.65V. Key facts driving the architecture: measured nfet_01v8 Vth~0.5-0.6V and pfet_01v8 |Vth|~0.9-1.05V with muCox_p/muCox_n ~ 23/100 uA/V^2, so a PMOS diode load or PMOS second stage needs its drain/gate node below ~0.4V, incompatible with the NMOS input pair tail node at Vin=1.1V. Design: (1) Complementary NMOS + PMOS input pairs. Each pair is folded to the shared high-impedance node B via its own NMOS bottom current mirror, while the pair drains are held by PMOS current sources whose sources sit at VDD (no threshold stacking), giving a first-stage output that sits naturally at ~0.9V; this covers 0.25-1.1V without a threshold dead-zone. (2) Second stage: NMOS common source (gate at node B) with a PMOS current-source load, so the output swings 0.25-1.1V at VDD=1.35V. (3) Current-density matching: mirror diode W/L and second-stage W/L are ratioed to their branch currents so the mirror node A, first-stage output B and second-stage gate sit at the same Vgs at every corner; this removes the systematic offset and gives <=3.6mV follower error over all 9 PVTs and 2-5pF loads. (4) Compensation: 5pF Miller cap plus 1.5k nulling resistor (1/gm of stage 2) placing the RHP zero above the UGF; loop gain 72-77dB and first unity crossing 10-16MHz. (5) All bias from the 10uA IBIAS diode with separate 10uA NMOS-mirrored PMOS reference branches for the P-tail (10uA/side pair), input-pair current sources (30uA each) and output load (120uA); supply-independent currents and full IBIAS compliance. Resources: 2580um^2 MOS W*L (<3000), 5pF explicit C (<=10pF), 151 elements, m<=28. Verified on the complete 210-case public grid: DC tracking 0.57-3.57mV (<=10mV), power 278-404uW (<=600uW), loop gain 72-76.6dB (>=50dB), UGF 10.0-16.4MHz (>=3MHz), no 0dB recross, input noise 51-96uVrms 10Hz-10MHz (<=150uV), small-step settling <=242ns (<=500ns) with overshoot <=7.0% (<=10%), large-step settling <=307ns (<=1us) and slew 4.0-7.6V/us (>=2V/us), PSRR 78-86dB@1kHz/46-53dB@1MHz, CMRR 70-77dB, input DC current ~0, IBIAS compliant." 2>&1 | tail -20

MODEL
2:43:48

Design submitted and frozen (dut-78078fea7b6e8a2b306ef8b3). Session complete.

Summary

Task: design a continuous-time OTA (single netlist, fixed ports) meeting DC tracking, bandwidth, step, noise, PSRR, CMRR and power limits over 9 PVT corners and 2–5 pF loads.

How I worked: no local ngspice, so the client was the only simulator. I first made v1 from theory, then used the client's reports to reverse-engineer the actual SKY130 device behavior — critically, by building probe DUTs (diode/resistor networks) whose port power reveals device Vgs/Vsg. That showed the decisive asymmetry: NFET Vth ≈ 0.5–0.6 V but PFET |Vth| ≈ 0.9–1.05 V with µCox_p/µCox_n ≈ 23/100 µA/V².

Key design decisions:

  1. Complementary (N + P) input pairs — obligatory, since neither polarity alone spans 0.25–1.1 V input CM.
  2. NMOS bottom current mirror per pair, with the pair drains held by PMOS current sources referenced to VDD. The naive 5T-with-PMOS-load (v1/v2) fails because a PMOS diode/second-stage needs its node < VDD−1.0 V, colliding with the NMOS pair's tail node at Vin = 1.1 V. Folding to NMOS mirrors puts all nodes at ~Vgs_n, which is naturally compatible.
  3. NMOS common-source second stage with a PMOS current-source load (only this can swing 0.25–1.1 V at VDD = 1.35 V given the weak PFET).
  4. Current-density matching (mirror W/L vs its branch current = output-device W/L vs its current) so the mirror diode node, first-stage output and 2nd-stage gate sit at identical Vgs at every corner — this killed the systematic offset (DC tracking 0.57–3.6 mV vs 10 mV limit).
  5. Miller Cc = 5 pF + 1.5 kΩ nulling resistor (≈1/gm of stage 2) to push the RHP zero above the UGF; also tuned tail current/load current so PM ≥ 70° in all corners.

Verified over the full 210-case public grid: DC ≤3.6 mV, loop gain 72–77 dB, UGF 10–16 MHz (no 0 dB recross), noise 51–96 µV, small-step settling ≤242 ns with ≤7.0 % overshoot, large-step settling ≤307 ns with slew 4.0–7.6 V/µs, PSRR 78–86 dB@1 kHz / 46–53 dB@1 MHz, CMRR 70–77 dB, power 278–404 µW, 2580 µm² MOS area, 5 pF explicit C.
